# 2048 witness: a legal play of the standard 4x4 game.
# The 32,781-move line to the 131072 tile: the fewest moves in
# which the largest tile of 4x4 2048 can be built (paper/main.pdf,
# Theorem 4). Every spawn is a 4 until the final fifteen moves.
# Check it with:  python3 verify/verify2048.py witness/131072.txt
# Lines: 'start ROW COL VALUE' for the two starting tiles, then one
# move per line: 'DIR ROW COL VALUE' = slide DIR (U/R/D/L), then the
# new tile that appeared at (ROW, COL) with VALUE. Row 0 is the top row,
# column 0 the left column.
